### Continuous piecewise optimizer details
We can compute continuous score exactly with full discrete on M (it is discrete, but small). We'll choose M=2048 or 1024. Since seed length n=6121, using M=2048 maybe closer. M=2048 FFT size 4096 for conv; gradient step eval maybe ~0.1ms. Good.

Piecewise optimizer `_piecewise_optimize(M,K,budget_s,rng,tag)`:
- Initialize weights list several profiles:
  - uniform
  - random Dirichlet alpha 0.5/1/2
  - sinusoidal: w_s ∝ 1 + amplitude * cos(2π m s/K + phase), ensure positive (clip). For m odd/even? We can allow any, nonneg of w.
  - beta/gamma: w_s ∝ (s/K)^a (1-s/K)^b
  - alternating: [1,2,1,2,...]? etc.
- For each init, run coordinate descent for a fixed number of iterations/time until budget split. We can interleave inits and keep best.
- Represent weights normalized sum1, positive. If weight tiny, set epsilon.
- Evaluate score of profile. Use function `_pc_eval(weights,M,K)` returns score,c,maxc,a.
- Coordinate step:
  - Compute a and c. maxc.
  - T = maxc * max(0.02, 0.2 / (it+1)?) start 0.1, min 1e-4.
  - p=softmax((c-maxc)/T), with clipping.
  - grad_a=`_derivative_c(p,a)` (2 corr). We don't need temp factor; it's gradient of ∑p c.
  - seg_grad = mean over segment: since a uniform, can compute by indexing. For each seg s, sum(grad_a[start:end]) / len.
  - Select donors: top maxD segments by seg_grad with weight > eps. Receivers: bottom minD with weight > eps and not donors.
  - Candidate fractions: [0.01,0.03,0.08,0.2,0.4,0.7,0.95] maybe. For each pair, transfer fraction of donor weight: w_d*(1-f), w_r += f*w_d. Normalize after? This keeps sum.
  - Evaluate score for candidates; choose best if score < best_iter*(1-1e-9). To avoid many evals, maxD/minD=4 =>28 pairs*7=196 evals, okay.
  - If no improvement and no_improve>10, perturb weights: multiply by exp(sigma*randn) with sigma 0.1-0.5 or mix with random. Also reset T larger.
  - Keep best weights/score.
- Time loop. At end return best profile a (normalized to M). Then interpolation to n.

Could K=32, pairs 28, evals200; each eval M=2048 FFT 4096 ~maybe 0.2ms? 40s. We'll limit continuous total time 90s.

But is piecewise coordinate over weights enough to optimize within segments? The profile within a segment is constant; full local can refine. Good.

### Full optimizer time allocation and restarting
In `_gradient_optimize`, coordinate polish is called when no accepts for some time, but also we can call it at end. The full optimizer itself may spend too long in coordinate polish (previous budget 0.25-0.7s each call). We can allocate.

Pseudo:
```
def _gradient_optimize(f0, budget_s, rng, tag):
    f=normalize(f0); n=f.size; c=autocorr(f); maxc=c.max(); best...
    deadline=time.monotonic()+budget_s
    temp_rel=0.1; min=1e-6
    no_accept=0; last_coord=0
    while now<deadline:
       # gradient step
       ... accept?
       if accepted: temp_rel *=0.99
       else:
          no_accept +=1
          # every 0.5s or no_accept>10 call coordinate polish with budget 1-3s
          if now-last_coord > coord_interval or no_accept > 15:
             coord_b = 2.0 if n<8000 else 3.0 maybe cap remaining
             fp,sc,cp = _coordinate_polish(f,c,coord_b,rng,tag+'coord')
             if sc < maxc_best? update; f=fp;c=cp;maxc=...
             # if no improvement, perturb after many fails
          if no_accept > 60: perturb
       # progress print every 30s?
    final coordinate polish short
    return best
```
Need avoid nested variable in while causing too many prints. Use print tag maybe every 60s.

### Detailed code for piecewise `_pc_eval`
```
def _pc_eval(weights, M, K):
    w = np.asarray(weights, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    w[~np.isfinite(w)] = 0.0
    np.maximum(w, 1e-12, out=w)
    s = w.sum()
    if s <= 0: w = np.full(K,1.0)
    else: w /= s
    lens = np.empty(K)
    base = M // K
    rem = M - base*K
    # ensure rem<K; if K > M? use K=min(K,M)
    for idx... but avoid loop? Use vector: lens = base + (np.arange(K)<rem)
    a = np.repeat(w / lens, lens.astype(int))? `np.repeat` with lengths array.
    c = _autocorr(a)
    mx = float(c.max())
    return float(2*M*mx), a, c, w
```

def _softmax_weights_from_c(c, maxc, T):

```

#### `_autocorr(f)`
```
def _autocorr(f):
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64)
    n=f.size
    if n==0: return empty
    m=2*n-1
    size=_fft_size(m)
    F=np.fft.rfft(f,size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(F*F, size)
    # due to floating, small negative; leave; max fine.
    return c[:m]
```
Could `F*F` produce negative? no but due complex? F real transform; square complex, inverse real. Good.

#### `_normalize`
- If sum >0, divide; else uniform. It clips negative to0, inf to0.

#### `_project_simplex`
Previous algorithm for simplex x>=0,sum=1. Use it. Ensure for v size n large sorting O(n log n) maybe okay few times. But Euclidean step line search may call many times; sorting 6000 each could be heavy. Mirror step normalization cheaper (no sort). We can use Euclidean less often. Keep.

### Full gradient details in code
```
def _gradient_optimize(f0,budget_s,rng,tag=""):
    f = _normalize(f0)
    n=f.size
    if n<2: return f, ...
    c = _autocorr(f)
    maxc = float(c.max())
    best_score = float(2*n*maxc)
    best_f = f.copy()
    deadline = time.monotonic() + budget_s
    temp_rel = 0.15
    min_temp_rel = 1e-6
    no_accept=0
    last_coord = 0.0
    coord_interval=0.7 if n<2000 else 0.9 if n<10000 else 1.2
    coord_budget = 1.5 if n<2000 else 2.5 if n<12000 else 3.5
    it=0
    start_best = best_score
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       it+=1
       if maxc <=0: break
       T = maxc * temp_rel
       # p softmax
       z = (c - maxc)/T; clip; p=exp; p/=sum
       g = _weighted_gradient(p,f)
       gm = g - float(g.mean())
       scale = float(np.max(np.abs(gm)))
       accepted=False
       if scale > 1e-14:
          best_mc=maxc; best_ft=None; best_ct=None
          # Scale gm to unit max. Multipliers:
          for e in (0.02,0.08,0.3,1.0,3.0):
             # log step
             h = gm / scale
             # move opposite gradient: f*exp(-e*h)
             arg = -e * h; clip -20,20; ft=f*np.exp(arg); ft=_normalize(ft)
             ct=_autocorr(ft); mc=float(ct.max()) ...
          for e in (0.05,0.3,1.0):
              ft=_project_simplex(f - e*(gm/scale)); ft=_normalize(ft); ct=...
          if best_ct is not None and best_mc < maxc - 1e-15:
             f=best_ft; c=best_ct; maxc=best_mc; accepted=True
       if accepted:
          no_accept=0; score=2n*maxc; update best; temp_rel=max(min,temp_rel*0.99)
       else:
          no_accept +=1
          now=time.monotonic()
          # coordinate polish if time
          if now-last_coord > coord_interval or no_accept > 20:
             cb = min(coord_budget, max(0.5, deadline - now - 5))
             if cb > 0.3:
                fp,sc,cp = _coordinate_polish(f,c,cb,rng,tag)
                if sc < best_score or sc < 2n*maxc - 1e-12:
                   ... accepted=True; no_accept=0; last_coord=now
                else:
                   if no_accept > 70 or (it and it%20000==0): perturb
                   # increase temp
                   temp_rel = min(0.35, temp_rel*1.7 + 1e-4)
          else:
             temp_rel=max(min_temp_rel,temp_rel*0.995)
       # maybe every 20s print
    # final short coordinate from current/best
    final_time=max(0, deadline-time.monotonic())
    if final_time>1: ... coordinate polish
    return best_f,best_score
```
Need handle if `_coordinate_polish` returns c None? It should.
- If coordinate polish improves best but not current, we might set current to best to continue. Yes.
- Perturb from best_f: `f = _perturb(best_f, f, rng)`; recompute c. But after perturb, current max may be worse; gradient may escape. Good.
- Ensure not infinite loops; time check.

Coordinate polish itself may call `_normalize` and recompute c. It can be expensive but okay.

### Coordinate polish code specifics
```
def _coordinate_polish(f0,c0,budget_s,rng,tag=""):
    f=_normalize(f0)
    n=f.size
    if n<2 or budget_s<=0: return ...
    L=2*n-1
    c=_autocorr(f) if invalid else c0.copy
    maxc=float(c.max()); best_score=2n*maxc; best_f=f.copy()
    deadline=time.monotonic()+budget_s
    active_rel=0.05
    min_active_rel=1e-4
    no_improve=0
    move_count=0
    D=8 if n<=8000 else 6; R=D
    fracs=(0.05,0.15,0.35,0.7,0.98)
    while now<deadline and no_improve<25:
        maxc=float(c.max())
        T=maxc*active_rel
        p=softmax(c,T)
        g=_weighted_gradient(p,f)
        # If p all uniform? okay.
        donors = argsort(g)[::-1] first D with f>eps
        recv = argsort(g) first R not in donors and f maybe any
        if none: no_improve +=1; break
        # precompute units
        units_d=[]; for i donors: u=np.zeros(L); u[i:i+n] = -f; append
        units_r=[]; for j recv: u=np.zeros(L); u[j:j+n] = f; append
        best_mc=maxc; best_move=None
        for i,ui in ...:
          fi=f[i]
          for j,uj in ...:
             if i==j continue
             # candidate dvals
             cand=set()
             for f_frac in fracs: dv=fi*f_frac; add if <=fi
             diff=fi-f[j]
             if diff>1e-12:
                cand.add(0.5*diff); cand.add(0.1*diff)
             # also small random? no here
             for dv in cand:
                if dv<=1e-14: continue
                delta.fill(0); np.add(ui,uj,out=delta); delta*=2*dv; q=dv*dv; if 2j<L: delta[2j]+=q; if 2i<L: delta[2i]+=q; if i+j<L: delta[i+j]-=2q
                np.add(c, delta, out=total); mc=float(total.max())
                if mc < best_mc - 1e-15:
                   best_mc=mc; best_move=(i,j,dv,delta.copy())
        # random fallback pairs 15
        if best_move is None or best_mc >= maxc - 1e-15:
            for _ in range(15):
              i=rng.integers(0,n); j=...
              if i==j or f[i]<=eps continue
              dv = f[i]*rng.uniform(0.02,0.5)
              # evaluate via helper _transfer_max; need delta; total
              delta.fill(0); delta[i:i+n]-=f; delta[j:j+n]+=f; delta*=2dv; q...; add c total; mc...
              if improve update
        if best_move is None or best_mc >= maxc -1e-15:
             no_improve +=1
             if no_improve > 10:
                # perturb current from best with small noise and recompute c
                f = _perturb(best_f, f, rng)
                c = _autocorr(f); maxc=float(c.max()); active_rel = max(active_rel,0.1)
                continue
             continue
        i,j,dv,d_saved=best_move
        f[i]-=dv; f[j]+=dv
        # clean tiny negatives
        if f[i] < 0: f[i]=0; f[j]+=f[i]? avoid
        c = c + d_saved
        move_count+=1
        no_improve=0
        cur_score=2n*best_mc
        if cur_score < best_score:
             best_score=cur_score; best_f=f.copy()
        # recompute if drift every 30
        if move_count%30==0:
             f=_normalize(f); c=_autocorr(f); maxc=float(c.max()); cur=...; update best
        active_rel = max(min_active_rel, active_rel*0.97)
    # final recompute exact
    f=_normalize(f); c=_autocorr(f); sc=...
    if sc <= best_score: return f,sc,c
    best_f=_normalize(best_f); bc=_autocorr(best_f); return best_f,...
```
Potential issue: p weights sum1; `_weighted_gradient` returns derivative of ∑p c (not LSE). Good.
- `total` array preallocated length L; after `np.add(c, delta, out=total)`, if we saved delta, c not modified. For apply, c = c + d_saved. Good.
- If no_improve many, perturb could make current score worse. We keep best. active_rel increase. No_improve maybe reset after perturb? yes to 0 to allow tries.
- No closure.

### Perturb function
```
def _perturb(best_f, current_f, rng):
    n=best_f.size
    r=random()
    if r<0.35: sigma=rng.uniform(0.05,0.8); f=best_f*np.exp(sigma*rng.standard_normal(n)); return normalize
    elif r<0.65: alpha=rng.uniform(0.05,0.35); noise=rng.random(n); normalize and mix
    elif r<0.85: smooth window max(3,n//200); noise
    else: # active-targeted? create f=best_f; choose few random coordinates with large weight and reduce? 
       z = np.zeros(n); idx=random permutation? Maybe just multiplicative larger.
    return normalize
```
Need no lambda. `np.random.default_rng` seeded.

### Cross-check coordinate gradient direction with p one-hot lag. Suppose active high c_k. g_i=2a_{k-i}. For k center, g high for coordinates with complementary a high. `_fft_size`:
```
def _fft_size(m):
    if m <= 2: return 2
    return 1 << (int(m)-1).bit_length()
```
For m=3, (2).bit_length=2, 1<<2=4. Good.

`_normalize` as above.

`_autocorr`:
```
def _autocorr(f):
    f=np.asarray(f,dtype=np.float64)
    n=f.size
    if n==0: return np.empty(0)
    # ensure nonnegative? clip? Maybe not, but for safety
    f=np.maximum(f,0.0)
    m=2*n-1
    size=_fft_size(m)
    F=np.fft.rfft(f,size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(F*F, size)
    return c[:m]
```
Clipping might change if f has tiny negative; fine. But if normalizing elsewhere, ok.

`_fast_score`:
```
def _fast_score(f):
    f=_normalize(f)
    if f.size==0: return np.inf
    c=_autocorr(f)
    if c.size==0: return np.inf
    return float(2.0*f.size*float(c.max()))
```

`_weighted_gradient`:
```
def _weighted_gradient(p,f):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64); f=np.asarray(f,dtype=np.float64)
    n=f.size; L=p.size
    if n==0: return empty
    if L==0: return zeros
    size=_fft_size(L+n-1)
    P=np.fft.rfft(p,size)
    F=np.fft.rfft(f,size)
    corr=np.fft.irfft(P*np.conj(F), size)
    out=corr[:n]
    # tiny negative? keep
    return 2.0*out
```
The derivative is p sum arbitrary. If p uniform and f sum1, gradient sum? ∑_i g_i = 2∑_i∑_k p_k f_{k-i} =2∑_k p_k ∑_i f_{k-i}. Sum over valid i maybe number? Not necessarily 2. Centering okay.

`_project_simplex` as previous. Ensure if v contains nan, set 0.

`_softmax_p(c, maxc, T)`:
```
def _softmax_p(c,maxc,T):
    if T<=0 or maxc<=0:
       p=np.zeros_like(c); idx=np.argmax(c); p[idx]=1; return p
    z=(c-maxc)/T
    np.clip(z,-45,0,out=z)
    p=np.exp(z)
    s=p.sum()
    if s<=1e-300:
       top=np.argmax(c); p.fill(0); p[top]=1
    else: p/=s
    return p
```
For many max ties, exp(0)=1 for all with z close0, good.

`_score_from_c(c,n)`: return 2*n*maxc. Maybe top-level.

`_interp_to_length`:
```
def _interp_to_length(f,n):
   f=_normalize(f)
   m=f.size
   if n<=0: empty
   if n==1: [1]
   if m==1: ones(n)
   if m==n: return f
   x_old=np.linspace(0,m-1,m); x_new=np.linspace(0,n-1,n)
   return _normalize(np.interp(x_new,x_old,f))
```

`_smooth_with_window`: same as previous.

```
def _fourier_seed(n,rng,amp=0.7,n_modes=6):
    if n==0...
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    u=np.zeros(n)
    u += amp*rng.uniform(-1,1)*(x-0.5)
    for mode in (1,2,3,5,7,11): maybe up to n_modes:
       coeff = amp*0.35*rng.uniform(-1,1)
       phase=rng.uniform(0,2pi)
       u += coeff*np.cos(2np.pi*mode*x+phase)
       # perhaps sin for odd?
    f=np.exp(0.8*u)
    f=np.maximum(f,1e-6); return normalize
```
Need no lambda. Use `math.pi` or `np.pi`.

f=(x+0.01)**alpha * (1-x+0.01)**beta
return normalize

def _try_add_seed(seeds,name,f,n):
    f=_interp_to_length(f,n)
    try: sc=_fast_score(f)
    except: return
    if np.isfinite(sc): seeds.append((float(sc),len(seeds),str(name),f))
```

### Piecewise functions top-level
`_pc_profile(weights,M,K)`:
```
def _pc_profile(weights,M,K):
    K=int(K); M=int(M)
    if K<=0: K=1
    if M<=0: return np.empty(0)
    K=min(K,M)
    w=np.asarray(weights,dtype=np.float64).copy().reshape(-1)
    if w.size<K: w=np.pad(w,(0,K-w.size),constant_values=1e-6)
    elif w.size>K: w=w[:K]
    w[~np.isfinite(w)]=0.0
    np.minimum? maximum
    w=np.maximum(w,1e-12)
    s=w.sum()
    if s<=1e-300: w=np.full(K,1.0)
    else: w/=s
    base=M//K; rem=M-base*K
    lens=np.full(K,base,dtype=np.int64)
    if rem: lens[:rem]+=1
    vals=w/lens.astype(np.float64)
    return np.repeat(vals,lens)
```

`_pc_eval(weights,M,K)` returns score, f, c.
```
f=_pc_profile(...); c=_autocorr(f); mx=...; return 2M*mx,f,c
```

`_piecewise_optimize(M,K,budget_s,rng,tag)`:
Need generate initial weights and loop. To avoid inner helper, we can write all in one function with loops but no nested functions.
- `inits=[]`
```
# uniform
inits.append(np.ones(K))
# random
for _ in range(5): inits.append(rng.dirichlet(np.full(K,1.0)))
# sinusoids
for mode in (2,3,5):
   for amps in (0.5,0.8,1.0):
      s=np.arange(K)/K
      w=1.0+amps*np.cos(2*np.pi*mode*s + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
      w=np.maximum(w,1e-3); inits.append(w)
# beta endpoints
for a,b in [(0.5,2),(2,0.5),(0.2,0.2),(2,2)]:
   s=np.linspace(0.2,0.8,K)
   w=s**a*(1-s)**b; inits.append(w)
# alternating
alt=np.ones(K); alt[::2]*=2; alt[1::2]*=0.5? inits.append
```
Limit inits maybe 12.
- We can distribute budget among inits: `time_per = max(1.0, budget_s / max(1,len(inits)))` but not exceed 5 maybe. For K=16, 12 inits*? too many. Instead we can interleave: maintain a list of states (weights,best_score,init_index), and each iteration pick next state, do one coordinate descent iteration. This is more complex but doable without nested functions.
Simpler: Run a fixed number of coordinate descent iterations per init `iters=int(min(20, max(3, budget_s/ (len(inits)*0.2))))` but time-bound. Since piecewise eval cheap, we can do all inits to exhaustion? For M=2048,K=32, each coordinate iteration ~70 evals; 70*FFT 4096. 50 iters ~3500 evals maybe 0.5s. 12 inits ~6s. Fine. We can allocate time per K.
Pseudo:
```
best_w=None; best_score=inf; best_f=None
for init_idx,w in enumerate(inits):
   if time.monotonic()+2 > deadline: break
   ww=w.copy()
   score,f,c = _pc_eval(ww,M,K)
   best_local=score; best_local_w=ww; best_local_f=f
   no_improve=0
   active_rel=0.1; min_rel=1e-4
   while time.monotonic() < deadline - 0.2 and no_improve < 25:
       # ensure ww normalized
       score,f,c = _pc_eval(ww,M,K)
       if score < best_local: update
       maxc=score/(2M)
       T=maxc*active_rel
       p=_softmax_p(c,maxc,T)
       grad=_weighted_gradient(p,f)
       seg_grad=np.array([mean(grad[start:end]) for ...])  # loop K
       donors top 4, recv bottom 4
       best_move...
       # candidates
       for d in donors:
         for r in receivers:
          for frac in fracs:
             w_new=ww.copy(); transfer; sc2,_,_= _pc_eval(w_new,M,K)
             if improve best
       if best_move None:
          no_improve+=1
          if no_improve>4: perturb ww
          else: break
          if no_improve>8: break init
       else: ww=best_w; active_rel*=0.95; no_improve=0
   if best_local < best_score: update best
```
This is nested? It's just code in function, no nested functions. Loops using Python list comprehension? Comprehensions not closures okay, but to be safe use explicit loops for seg_grad.
- We need segment starts: `starts = np.concatenate?` Could precompute in function.
```
starts=[]; idx=0; for s in range(K): starts.append(idx); idx += lens[s]`
lens computed. For seg_grad loop `seg_grad.append(np.mean(grad[starts[s]:starts[s+1]])`.
To avoid repeated lens in each iteration, compute once. But K constant.
- Candidate transfer function? We can inline.
- `w_new = ww.copy()` inside many loops costly but K small.
- The `_pc_eval` normalizes weights each time; okay.

This piecewise optimizer can be time-consuming if K large and many inits; but okay.

### Full block scale optimizer
`_block_scale_optimize(f0,K,budget_s,rng,tag)`:
- fbase = normalize(f0), n. K=min(K,n). Compute lens/starts same as piecewise contiguous.
- base_sums: loop.
- If any sum < eps, set fbase maybe add tiny to block? For scaling, if base_sums[s]==0, any w_s can't move because shape zero? We can replace base_sums with eps and fbase block values maybe uniform over block to give shape. Simpler: if block sum < 1e-8, set fbase in that block to 1/(n*K?)? Actually we can adjust fbase: add small mass to block. For code: `base_sums_safe = np.where(base_sums < 1e-8, 1e-8, base_sums)`.
- Initial w=base_sums/base sum.
- current_f = fbase (since alpha=1). But if base_sums sanitized different, alpha not exactly? We can compute `alpha=base_sums_safe/base_sums_safe`; no change. Good.
- In loop, `w` sum1. To build a: Need block index array to multiply. Precompute `block_index=np.repeat(np.arange(K), lens)`. `alpha = w / base_sums_safe`. `a = fbase * alpha[block_index]`; normalize. (Since alpha[0]=1, sum approx1; normalize fixes.)
- Evaluate score. If improve best update.
- Compute p, grad, block_grad mean (or weighted mean). Donors high, receivers low.
- Candidate transfers (4 donors*4*5). Choose best score, update w, f? We can just update w and recompute f next loop; but for candidate evaluation, compute each a. To save, after best move set w=best_w and `current_f` can remain until next loop.
- Perturb weights: multiply by exp(sigma randn) normalize, or mix random.
- Return best sequence.

This can be used as seed improvement: after seed pool build, for top 2 seeds, run block K=16,32 for 10-20s each and add to pool. But full block eval at n large maybe moderate. Maybe skip to keep code shorter? It adds diversity. Include but limit time.

### Seed pool building with time
Top-level `_build_seed_pool(f0,n,rng,seed_budget)`:
- `seeds=[]`
- Add base seeds.
- If seed_budget>60: run piecewise optimizations.
But if we run block optimizer inside, maybe use seed_budget.
Let's outline:
```
def _build_seed_pool(f0,n,rng,seed_budget):
    seeds=[]
    # base seeds no extra n
    _try_add_seed(seeds,'given',f0,n)
    _try_add_seed(seeds,'rev',f0[::-1],n)
    for win in (5,11,31,101,251): ...
    for gamma in (0.6,0.85,1.2,1.6): ...
    _try_add_seed(seeds,'uniform',np.ones(n),n)
    for i in range(10): _try_add_seed(seeds,'fourier'+str(i),_fourier_seed(n,rng),n)
    for i in range(6): _try_add_seed(seeds,'beta'+str(i),_beta_seed(n,rng),n)
    for p,q in ((-0.5,1.5),(1.5,-0.5),(-1,0),(0,-1),(0.5,0.5)): add power_seed
    # endpoint boost
    eb=f0.copy(); eb[0]+=eb[0]+0.05; eb[-1]+=...; add; also center boost? 
    # piecewise
    if seed_budget > 45 and n > 0:
       time_left=seed_budget - 30
       configs=[(2048,16,18),(2048,32,18),(1024,16,14),(1024,32,14)]
       total_cfg=sum seconds
       if time_left>60:
          scale=time_left/total_cfg maybe, ensure 5 max 25
          for M,K,sec in configs:
             sec_scaled=min(25,max(5,sec*scale))
             if time.monotonic() + sec_scaled + 5 < seed_deadline:

### `_perturb`
```
def _perturb(best_f, current_f, rng):
    best_f=_normalize(best_f)
    n=best_f.size
    r=float(rng.random())
    if r<0.40:
       sigma=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.8))
       z=rng.standard_normal(n)
       f=best_f*np.exp(sigma*z)
    elif r<0.70:
       alpha=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.40))
       noise=rng.random(n)
       ns=noise.sum()
       if ns>0: noise/=ns
       f=(1-alpha)*best_f + alpha*noise
    elif r<0.85:
       win=max(3,n//100)
       f=_smooth_with_window(best_f,win)
       sigma=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.5))
       f=f*np.exp(sigma*rng.standard_normal(n))
    else:
       f=best_f.copy()
       # reverse halves perturb? create mixture
       rev=best_f[::-1]
       alpha=float(rng.uniform(0.1,0.5))
       f=(1-alpha)*f+alpha*rev
       sigma=float(rng.uniform(0.02,0.3))
       f=f*np.exp(sigma*rng.standard_normal(n))
    return _normalize(f)
```
No lambda.

def _build_seed_pool(f0,n,rng,seed_budget):
    deadline=time.monotonic()+max(1.0,float(seed_budget))
    seeds=[]

        total=...

           scale=min(1.5, max(0.3, (remaining-30)/total))

    return seeds

`_normalize`:
```
def _normalize(v):
    v=np.asarray(v,dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if v.size==0:
       return np.ones(1,dtype=np.float64)
    v[~np.isfinite(v)] = 0.0
    np.maximum(v,0.0,out=v)
    s=float(v.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
       return np.ones(v.size,dtype=np.float64)/float(v.size)
    v /= s
    return v
```

```
def _fourier_seed(n,rng,amp=0.8,n_modes=6):
    if n<=0: return np.empty(0)
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    u=np.zeros(n)
    u += amp*float(rng.uniform(-1,1))*(x-0.5)
    modes=[1,2,3,5,8,13]
    for idx,m in enumerate(modes[:max(1,int(n_modes))]):
        coeff=0.35*amp*float(rng.uniform(-1,1))
        phase=float(rng.uniform(0.0,2*np.pi))
        u += coeff*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*x + phase)
        u += coeff*0.8*np.sin(2*np.pi*m*x + phase)
    f=np.exp(0.75*u)
    np.maximum(f,1e-8,out=f)
    return _normalize(f)
```
Good.

`_power_seed`:
```
def _power_seed(n, alpha,beta):
    if n<=0: empty
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    f=(x+0.02)**(float(alpha))*(1.0-x+0.02)**float(beta)
    np.maximum(f,1e-8)
    return _normalize(f)
```

def _piecewise_optimize(M,K,budget_s,rng,tag=""):
    deadline=time.monotonic()+max(1.0,float(budget_s))
    if M<=0 or K<=0: return None, np.inf
    K=int(min(K,M))
    base=M//K; rem=M-base*K
    lens=np.full(K,base,dtype=np.int64); if rem: lens[:rem]+=1
    # Build inits list
    inits=[]
    inits.append(np.ones(K))
    for _ in range(4): inits.append(rng.dirichlet(np.full(K,1.0)))
    s=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,K)
    for mode in (2,3,5):
       for ampl in (0.5,0.8,1.0):
          w=1.0+ampl*np.cos(2*np.pi*mode*s + float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)))
          np.maximum(w,0.05,out=w); inits.append(w)
    for a,b in ((0.5,2.0),(2.0,0.5),(0.3,0.3),(1.8,1.8)):

    alt=np.ones(K); alt[::2]=1.6; alt[1::2]=0.4; inits.append(alt)
    inits = inits[:14]

       local_best_score=np.inf; local_best_w=ww.copy(); local_best_f=None

           grad=_weighted_gradient(p,fa)

              start=sidx*? not because lens variable

### `_block_scale_optimize` code
```
def _block_scale_optimize(f0,K,budget_s,rng,tag=""):
   fbase=_normalize(f0); n=fbase.size
   if n<4 or K<2 or budget_s<=0: return fbase, _fast_score(fbase)
   deadline=...
   K=int(min(K,n))
   lens... starts...
   base_sums=np.empty(K)
   for s... base_sums[s]=float(fbase[starts[s]:starts[s+1]].sum())
   base_safe=np.where(base_sums<1e-10, 1e-10, base_sums)
   ww=base_sums/base_sums.sum()
   block_index=np.repeat(np.arange(K), lens)
   best_f=fbase.copy(); best_score=_fast_score(fbase)
   best_w=ww.copy()
   active_rel=0.08
   no_improve=0
   fracs=(0.05,0.2,0.4,0.7,0.95)
   while time.monotonic()<deadline-0.5 and no_improve<30:
      alpha=ww/base_safe
      fa=_normalize(fbase*alpha[block_index])
      c=_autocorr(fa); maxc=float(c.max()); score=float(2*n*maxc)
      if score < best_score: update best
      p=_softmax_p(c,maxc,maxc*active_rel)
      grad=_weighted_gradient(p,fa)
      seg_grad=np.empty(K); loop means
      d_order=argsort top4; r_order bottom4
      best_move None
      for d...
         wd=ww[d]
         if wd<=1e-8 continue
         for r...
            for frac...
               wnew=ww.copy(); wnew[d]=wd*(1-frac); wnew[r]+=wd*frac; wnew/=sum
               alpha_new=wnew/base_safe; a=_normalize(fbase*alpha_new[block_index]); c2=_autocorr(a); sc2=...
               if improve
      if best_move: ww=best_move; no_improve=0; active_rel=max(1e-4,active_rel*0.97)
      else: no_improve++; if no_improve>=15 break else perturb weights
   # final best eval
   alpha=best_w/base_safe; best_f=_normalize(fbase*alpha[block_index]); best_score=_fast_score(best_f)
   return best_f,best_score
```
Note `_fast_score` recomputes; okay.

### Potential performance: `_block_scale_optimize` at n=6121 and budget 20s. Each candidate eval size16384 FFT; 4*4*5=80 evals/iter. 80*1ms=0.08s; 200 iterations ~16s. Good. But Python overhead maybe 0.5s/iter =>100 iters. fine.

### In `_gradient_optimize`, coordinate polish uses `_coordinate_polish` which itself could spend budget. Need avoid runaway: coordinate_polish checks inside. Good.

### Testing gradient line search: If g scale zero, no move; no_accept leads coordinate.

### In `_gradient_optimize`, after coordinate polish, if it improved best but not current, we may want to set current to best_f to continue from best: If `sc < 2*n*maxc` (current) set f=fp,c=cp. If not but best improved, maybe keep current? For escaping, set current to best sometimes: if `sc < best_score_old`, and `sc < 2*n*maxc`? We can set if `sc < best_score` maybe. Code:
```
cur_score=2*n*maxc
if sc < cur_score -1e-12:
    f=fp; c=cp; maxc=sc/(2n); accepted=True
if sc < best_score:
    best_score=sc; best_f=fp.copy(); best_c=cp
```
If improved best but worse than current, not adopt current. But maybe we can adopt best to polish: if no_accept big, perturb from best anyway. Good.

### In `_coordinate_polish`, if current gets worse after perturb, it recomputes from f. It tracks best. Good.

### Potential issue: In `_gradient_optimize`, best_f normalized sum1, but best_c not used except return. At final, recompute.

```python
"""
Optimizer for the step-function objective

    score(a) = 2*n*max_k (a*a)_k / (sum a)^2

implemented with:
  - corrected FFT cross-correlation gradients for the active coefficients,
  - soft-max active-set mirror/Euclidean descent,
  - exact two-coordinate transfer polishing,
  - coarse continuous piecewise/block mass redistributions for good seeds,
  - restarts and full-budget final polishing.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math

TARGET_SCORE = 1.5027


def _fft_size(m):
    """Smallest power of two at least m and at least 2."""
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 2:
        return 2
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _normalize(v):
    """Project a real vector to non-negative values on the simplex."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if v.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    v[~np.isfinite(v)] = 0.0
    np.maximum(v, 0.0, out=v)
    s = float(v.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(v.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(v.size)
    v /= s
    return v


def _autocorr(f):
    """Linear convolution f*f of a non-negative vector, via FFT."""
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64)
    n = f.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    f = np.maximum(f, 0.0)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    size = _fft_size(m)
    F = np.fft.rfft(f, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(F * F, size)
    return c[:m]


def _fast_score(f):
    """Score for a normalized non-negative vector."""
    f = _normalize(f)
    if f.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    c = _autocorr(f)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * f.size * float(c.max()))


def _weighted_gradient(p, f):
    """
    Gradient of sum_k p_k c_k with respect to f.

    c_k = sum_i f_i f_{k-i}, so
    d c_k / d f_i = 2 f_{k-i} on valid indices.
    """
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64)
    n = f.size
    L = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if L == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    size = _fft_size(L + n - 1)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, size)
    F = np.fft.rfft(f, size)
    corr = np.fft.irfft(P * np.conj(F), size)
    return 2.0 * corr[:n]


def _project_simplex(v):
    """Euclidean projection onto x >= 0, sum x = 1."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v
    v[~np.isfinite(v)] = 0.0
    np.maximum(v, 0.0, out=v)
    s = float(v.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    cond = u * np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64) > (cssv - 1.0)
    if not np.any(cond):
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    rho = int(np.nonzero(cond)[0][-1])
    theta = (cssv[rho] - 1.0) / float(rho + 1.0)
    out = v - theta
    np.maximum(out, 0.0, out=out)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    out /= s
    return out


def _softmax_p(c, maxc, T):
    """Stable softmax over c with temperature T, centered at maxc."""
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    n = c.size
    if n == 0:
        return empty
    if maxc <= 0.0 or T <= 0.0:
        p = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        if n > 0:
            p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return p
    z = (c - maxc) / T
    np.clip(z, -45.0, 0.0, out=z)
    p = np.exp(z)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        p.fill(0.0)
        p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        p /= s
    return p


def _interp_to_length(f, n):
    """Linear resampling to length n and normalization."""
    f = _normalize(f)
    m = f.size
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    if m == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    if m == n:
        return f
    if m == 1:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, m - 1, m, dtype=np.float64)
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, n - 1, n, dtype=np.float64)
    return _normalize(np.interp(x1, x0, f))


def _smooth_with_window(f, win):
    """Uniform moving-average smoothing, then normalize."""
    f = _normalize(f)
    n = f.size
    if n <= 2:
        return f
    win = int(win)
    if win < 3:
        win = 3
    if win > n:
        win = max(3, n // 2)
    kernel = np.ones(win, dtype=np.float64) / float(win)
    return _normalize(np.convolve(f, kernel, mode="same"))


def _fourier_seed(n, rng, amp=0.8, n_modes=6):
    """Smooth random low-frequency bounded seed."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    u = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    u += amp * float(rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0)) * (x - 0.5)
    modes = (1, 2, 3, 5, 8, 13)
    for m in modes[:max(1, int(n_modes))]:
        coeff = 0.35 * amp * float(rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0))
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        u += coeff * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * x + phase)
        u += 0.75 * coeff * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * m * x + phase)
    f = np.exp(0.75 * u)
    np.maximum(f, 1e-8, out=f)
    return _normalize(f)


def _beta_seed(n, rng):
    """Asymmetric beta-shaped seed."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 3.0))
    b = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 3.0))
    f = (x + 0.005) ** a * (1.0 - x + 0.005) ** b
    np.maximum(f, 1e-12, out=f)
    return _normalize(f)


def _power_seed(n, alpha, beta):
    """Algebraic endpoint/center shape seed."""
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    f = (x + 0.02) ** float(alpha) * (1.0 - x + 0.02) ** float(beta)
    np.maximum(f, 1e-12, out=f)
    return _normalize(f)


def _try_add_seed(seeds, name, f, n):
    """Evaluate and append a seed if it is finite."""
    f = _interp_to_length(f, n)
    try:
        sc = _fast_score(f)
    except Exception:
        return
    if np.isfinite(sc):
        seeds.append((float(sc), len(seeds), str(name), f))


def _pc_profile(weights, M, K):
    """Piecewise constant profile of length M from K weights."""
    M = int(M)
    K = int(K)
    if M <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    K = max(1, min(K, M))

    w = np.asarray(weights, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1).copy()
    if w.size < K:
        w = np.pad(w, (0, K - w.size), mode="constant", constant_values=1e-6)
    elif w.size > K:
        w = w[:K]
    w[~np.isfinite(w)] = 0.0
    np.maximum(w, 1e-12, out=w)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        w = np.full(K, 1.0, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        w /= s

    base = M // K
    rem = M - base * K
    lens = np.full(K, base, dtype=np.int64)
    if rem > 0:
        lens[:rem] += 1
    vals = w / lens.astype(np.float64)
    return np.repeat(vals, lens)


def _pc_eval(weights, M, K):
    """Score, profile, convolution for a piecewise constant profile."""
    f = _pc_profile(weights, M, K)
    c = _autocorr(f)
    mx = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
    return float(2.0 * M * mx), f, c


def _piecewise_optimize(M, K, budget_s, rng, tag=""):
    """
    Optimize a short piecewise-constant seed by coarse block transfers.
    Returns (profile, score).
    """
    if M <= 0 or K <= 0 or budget_s <= 0.0:
        return None, np.inf

    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    M = int(M)
    K = int(min(K, M))

    base = M // K
    rem = M - base * K
    lens = np.full(K, base, dtype=np.int64)
    if rem > 0:
        lens[:rem] += 1
    starts = np.empty(K + 1, dtype=np.int64)
    starts[0] = 0
    for s in range(K):
        starts[s + 1] = starts[s] + lens[s]

    inits = []
    inits.append(np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64))
    for _ in range(4):
        inits.append(rng.dirichlet(np.full(K, 1.0, dtype=np.float64)))

    sgrid = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, K, dtype=np.float64)
    for mode in (2, 3, 5):
        for ampl in (0.5, 0.8, 1.0):
            w = 1.0 + ampl * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * mode * sgrid + float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)))
            np.maximum(w, 0.05, out=w)
            inits.append(w)
    for a, b in ((0.5, 2.0), (2.0, 0.5), (0.3, 0.3), (1.8, 1.8)):
        w = sgrid ** a * (1.0 - sgrid) ** b
        w = np.maximum(w, 0.05)
        inits.append(w)
    alt = np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64)
    alt[0::2] = 1.6
    alt[1::2] = 0.4
    inits.append(alt)
    inits = inits[:14]

    best_score = np.inf
    best_f = None
    fracs = (0.02, 0.08, 0.20, 0.40, 0.70, 0.95)

    for w0 in inits:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 1.0:
            break
        ww = np.asarray(w0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        ww = np.maximum(ww, 1e-8)
        ww /= ww.sum()

        local_best_score = np.inf
        local_best_w = ww.copy()
        local_best_f = None
        no_improve = 0
        active_rel = 0.10

        while time.monotonic() < deadline - 0.5 and no_improve < 35:
            score, fa, ca = _pc_eval(ww, M, K)
            if not np.isfinite(score):
                break
            if score < local_best_score:
                local_best_score = score
                local_best_w = ww.copy()
                local_best_f = fa.copy()

            maxc = score / max(1e-300, 2.0 * M)
            T = maxc * active_rel
            p = _softmax_p(ca, maxc, T)
            grad = _weighted_gradient(p, fa)

            seg_grad = np.empty(K, dtype=np.float64)
            for s in range(K):
                seg_grad[s] = float(np.mean(grad[starts[s]:starts[s + 1]]))

            donor_order = np.argsort(seg_grad)[::-1][:4]
            recv_order = np.argsort(seg_grad)[:4]
            recv_set = set(int(x) for x in recv_order)

            best_move = None
            best_move_score = score

            for d in donor_order:
                d = int(d)
                wd = float(ww[d])
                if wd <= 1e-8:
                    continue
                for r in recv_order:
                    r = int(r)
                    if r == d:
                        continue
                    for frac in fracs:
                        wnew = ww.copy()
                        wnew[d] = wd * (1.0 - frac)
                        wnew[r] = wnew[r] + wd * frac
                        wnew = np.maximum(wnew, 1e-10)
                        wnew /= wnew.sum()
                        sc2, _, _ = _pc_eval(wnew, M, K)
                        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_move_score - 1e-12:
                            best_move_score = sc2
                            best_move = wnew.copy()

            if best_move is not None:
                ww = best_move
                ww = np.maximum(ww, 1e-10)
                ww /= ww.sum()
                no_improve = 0
                active_rel = max(1e-4, active_rel * 0.97)
            else:
                no_improve += 1
                if no_improve >= 25:
                    break
                if no_improve >= 6:
                    ww = ww * np.exp(0.30 * rng.standard_normal(K))
                    ww = np.maximum(ww, 1e-8)
                    ww /= ww.sum()
                    active_rel = min(0.35, active_rel * 1.8 + 0.01)
                    no_improve = 0

        if np.isfinite(local_best_score) and local_best_score < best_score:
            best_score = local_best_score
            best_f = local_best_f

    if best_f is None:
        w = np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64)
        score, fa, ca = _pc_eval(w, M, K)
        best_f = fa
        best_score = score

    return _normalize(best_f), float(best_score)


def _block_scale_optimize(f0, K, budget_s, rng, tag=""):
    """
    Coarse full-length optimization by scaling contiguous blocks while
    preserving the local shape inside each block.
    """
    fbase = _normalize(f0)
    n = fbase.size
    K = int(K)
    if n < 4 or K < 2 or budget_s <= 0.0:
        return fbase, _fast_score(fbase)
    if K > n:
        K = n
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(1.0, float(budget_s))

    base = n // K
    rem = n - base * K
    lens = np.full(K, base, dtype=np.int64)
    if rem > 0:
        lens[:rem] += 1
    starts = np.empty(K + 1, dtype=np.int64)
    starts[0] = 0
    for s in range(K):
        starts[s + 1] = starts[s] + lens[s]

    base_sums = np.empty(K, dtype=np.float64)
    for s in range(K):
        base_sums[s] = float(fbase[starts[s]:starts[s + 1]].sum())
    base_sums = np.where(base_sums < 1e-12, 1e-12, base_sums)
    ww = base_sums / base_sums.sum()

    block_index = np.repeat(np.arange(K, dtype=np.int64), lens)

    best_f = fbase.copy()
    best_score = _fast_score(fbase)
    best_w = ww.copy()

    active_rel = 0.08
    no_improve = 0
    fracs = (0.05, 0.20, 0.40, 0.70, 0.95)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 0.5 and no_improve < 35:
        alpha = ww / base_sums
        fa = _normalize(fbase * alpha[block_index])
        c = _autocorr(fa)
        maxc = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
        score = float(2.0 * n * maxc)

        if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
            best_score = score
            best_f = fa.copy()
            best_w = ww.copy()

        if maxc <= 0.0:
            break

        p = _softmax_p(c, maxc, maxc * active_rel)
        grad = _weighted_gradient(p, fa)

        seg_grad = np.empty(K, dtype=np.float64)
        for s in range(K):
            seg_grad[s] = float(np.mean(grad[starts[s]:starts[s + 1]]))

        donor_order = np.argsort(seg_grad)[::-1][:4]
        recv_order = np.argsort(seg_grad)[:4]

        best_move = None
        best_move_score = score

        for d in donor_order:
            d = int(d)
            wd = float(ww[d])
            if wd <= 1e-8:
                continue
            for r in recv_order:
                r = int(r)
                if r == d:
                    continue
                for frac in fracs:
                    wnew = ww.copy()
                    wnew[d] = wd * (1.0 - frac)
                    wnew[r] = wnew[r] + wd * frac
                    wnew = np.maximum(wnew, 1e-12)
                    wnew /= wnew.sum()
                    an = _normalize(fbase * (wnew / base_sums)[block_index])
                    cn = _autocorr(an)
                    sc2 = float(2.0 * n * float(cn.max())) if cn.size else np.inf
                    if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_move_score - 1e-12:
                        best_move_score = sc2
                        best_move = wnew.copy()

        if best_move is not None:
            ww = best_move
            ww = np.maximum(ww, 1e-12)
            ww /= ww.sum()
            no_improve = 0
            active_rel = max(1e-4, active_rel * 0.97)
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve >= 30:
                break
            if no_improve >= 8:
                ww = ww * np.exp(0.25 * rng.standard_normal(K))
                ww = np.maximum(ww, 1e-8)
                ww /= ww.sum()
                active_rel = min(0.35, active_rel * 1.8 + 0.01)
                no_improve = 0

    alpha = best_w / base_sums
    best_f = _normalize(fbase * alpha[block_index])
    best_score = _fast_score(best_f)
    return best_f, float(best_score)


def _perturb(best_f, rng):
    """Random perturbations around a good state."""
    best_f = _normalize(best_f)
    n = best_f.size
    r = float(rng.random())

    if r < 0.40:
        sigma = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.85))
        z = rng.standard_normal(n)
        f = best_f * np.exp(sigma * z)
    elif r < 0.70:
        alpha = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.40))
        noise = rng.random(n)
        s = float(noise.sum())
        if s > 1e-300:
            noise /= s
        f = (1.0 - alpha) * best_f + alpha * noise
    elif r < 0.85:
        win = max(3, n // 120)
        f = _smooth_with_window(best_f, win)
        sigma = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.55))
        f = f * np.exp(sigma * rng.standard_normal(n))
    else:
        rev = best_f[::-1]
        alpha = float(rng.uniform(0.10, 0.50))
        f = (1.0 - alpha) * best_f + alpha * rev
        sigma = float(rng.uniform(0.02, 0.35))
        f = f * np.exp(sigma * rng.standard_normal(n))

    return _normalize(f)


def _coordinate_polish(f0, c0, budget_s, rng, tag=""):
    """
    Exact local search by single mass transfers between two coordinates.
    Keeps a delicate balance between focus-top lags and escape by noise.
    """
    f = _normalize(f0)
    n = f.size
    if n < 2 or budget_s <= 0.0:
        c = _autocorr(f)
        return f, float(2.0 * n * float(c.max())), c

    L = 2 * n - 1
    try:
        c = np.asarray(c0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        if c.size != L or not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
            c = _autocorr(f)
    except Exception:
        c = _autocorr(f)

    maxc = float(c.max())
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * maxc)
    best_f = f.copy()

    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    active_rel = 0.025
    min_active_rel = 1e-5
    no_improve = 0
    move_count = 0
    eps = 1e-14

    if n > 20000:
        D = 6
        R = 6
    else:
        D = 8
        R = 8

    fracs = (0.05, 0.15, 0.35, 0.65, 0.95)
    delta = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    total = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline and no_improve < 35:
        maxc = float(c.max())
        if maxc <= 0.0:
            break

        p = _softmax_p(c, maxc, maxc * active_rel)
        g = _weighted_gradient(p, f)

        donor_order = np.argsort(g)[::-1]
        recv_order = np.argsort(g)

        donors = []
        for idx in donor_order:
            idx = int(idx)
            if f[idx] > 1e-12 and len(donors) < D:
                donors.append(idx)

        donor_set = set(int(x) for x in donors)
        receivers = []
        for idx in recv_order:
            idx = int(idx)
            if idx not in donor_set and len(receivers) < R:
                receivers.append(idx)

        if not donors or not receivers:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve >= 15:
                f = _perturb(best_f, rng)
                c = _autocorr(f)
                maxc = float(c.max())
                active_rel = min(0.30, active_rel * 2.0 + 0.02)
                no_improve = 0
            continue

        d_units = []
        for i in donors:
            u = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            u[i:i + n] = -f
            d_units.append((int(i), u))

        r_units = []
        for j in receivers:
            u = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            u[j:j + n] = f
            r_units.append((int(j), u))

        best_mc = maxc
        best_move = None

        for i, ui in d_units:
            fi = float(f[i])
            if fi <= 1e-12:
                continue
            for j, uj in r_units:
                if i == j:
                    continue

                dvals = []
                for fr in fracs:
                    dvals.append(fi * fr)
                diff = fi - float(f[j])
                if diff > 1e-14:
                    dvals.append(0.5 * diff)
                    dvals.append(0.15 * diff)

                seen = set()
                for dv in dvals:
                    dv = float(dv)
                    if dv <= eps or dv >= fi * 0.999999:
                        continue
                    key = round(dv, 14)
                    if key in seen:
                        continue
                    seen.add(key)

                    delta.fill(0.0)
                    np.add(ui, uj, out=delta)
                    delta *= (2.0 * dv)
                    q = dv * dv
                    delta[2 * i] += q
                    delta[2 * j] += q
                    delta[i + j] -= 2.0 * q
                    np.add(c, delta, out=total)
                    mc = float(total.max())
                    if mc < best_mc - 1e-15:
                        best_mc = mc
                        best_move = (i, j, dv, delta.copy())

        # Random fallback transfers, useful when the active gradient is flat.
        if best_move is None or best_mc >= maxc - 1e-15:
            for _ in range(18):
                i = int(rng.integers(0, n))
                j = int(rng.integers(0, n))
                if i == j or f[i] <= 1e-12:
                    continue
                dv = float(f[i] * rng.uniform(0.02, 0.45))
                if dv <= eps or dv >= float(f[i]) * 0.999999:
                    continue

                delta.fill(0.0)
                delta[i:i + n] -= f
                delta[j:j + n] += f
                delta *= (2.0 * dv)
                q = dv * dv
                delta[2 * i] += q
                delta[2 * j] += q
                delta[i + j] -= 2.0 * q
                np.add(c, delta, out=total)
                mc = float(total.max())
                if mc < best_mc - 1e-15:
                    best_mc = mc
                    best_move = (i, j, dv, delta.copy())

        if best_move is None or best_mc >= maxc - 1e-15:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve >= 20:
                f = _perturb(best_f, rng)
                c = _autocorr(f)
                maxc = float(c.max())
                active_rel = min(0.30, active_rel * 2.0 + 0.02)
                no_improve = 0
            continue

        i, j, dv, d_saved = best_move
        dv = min(dv, float(f[i]) * 0.999999)
        if dv <= 0.0:
            f = _perturb(best_f, rng)
            c = _autocorr(f)
            maxc = float(c.max())
            no_improve = 0
            continue

        f[i] -= dv
        f[j] += dv
        c = c + d_saved
        move_count += 1
        no_improve = 0

        cur_score = float(2.0 * n * best_mc)
        if cur_score < best_score:
            best_score = cur_score
            best_f = f.copy()

        if move_count % 30 == 0:
            f = _normalize(f)
            c = _autocorr(f)
            maxc = float(c.max())
            cur_score = float(2.0 * n * maxc)
            if cur_score < best_score:
                best_score = cur_score
                best_f = f.copy()

        active_rel = max(min_active_rel, active_rel * 0.985)

    f = _normalize(f)
    c = _autocorr(f)
    sc = float(2.0 * n * float(c.max())) if c.size else np.inf

    if sc <= best_score:
        return f, sc, c

    bf = _normalize(best_f)
    bc = _autocorr(bf)
    bs = float(2.0 * n * float(bc.max())) if bc.size else np.inf
    return bf, bs, bc


def _gradient_optimize(f0, budget_s, rng, tag=""):
    """
    Soft active-set descent with periodic exact coordinate polishing.
    """
    f = _normalize(f0)
    n = f.size
    if n < 2 or budget_s <= 0.0:
        c = _autocorr(f)
        return f, float(2.0 * n * float(c.max()))

    c = _autocorr(f)
    maxc = float(c.max())
    best_f = f.copy()
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * maxc)

    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    temp_rel = 0.10
    min_temp_rel = 1e-7
    no_accept = 0
    it = 0
    last_coord = 0.0
    last_print = time.monotonic()

    if n < 3000:
        coord_interval = 0.55
        coord_budget = 1.2
    elif n < 12000:
        coord_interval = 0.80
        coord_budget = 2.2
    else:
        coord_interval = 1.10
        coord_budget = 3.2

    print(f"[{tag}] start best={best_score:.8f} n={n}")

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        now = time.monotonic()

        if maxc <= 0.0:
            break

        T = maxc * temp_rel
        p = _softmax_p(c, maxc, T)
        g = _weighted_gradient(p, f)
        gm = g - float(g.mean())
        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(gm)))

        accepted = False
        if scale > 1e-14:
            h = gm / scale
            best_mc = maxc
            best_ft = None
            best_ct = None

            mirror_steps = (0.03, 0.12, 0.45, 1.6, 5.0)
            for e in mirror_steps:
                arg = -e * h
                np.clip(arg, -32.0, 32.0, out=arg)
                ft = f * np.exp(arg)
                ft = _normalize(ft)
                ct = _autocorr(ft)
                mc = float(ct.max())
                if mc < best_mc:
                    best_mc = mc
                    best_ft = ft
                    best_ct = ct

            eucl_steps = (0.35, 1.8)
            for e in eucl_steps:
                ft = _project_simplex(f - e * h)
                ft = _normalize(ft)
                ct = _autocorr(ft)
                mc = float(ct.max())
                if mc < best_mc:
                    best_mc = mc
                    best_ft = ft
                    best_ct = ct

            if best_ct is not None and best_mc < maxc - 1e-15:
                f = best_ft
                c = best_ct
                maxc = best_mc
                accepted = True

        if accepted:
            no_accept = 0
            cur_score = float(2.0 * n * maxc)
            if cur_score < best_score:
                best_score = cur_score
                best_f = f.copy()
                if best_score < TARGET_SCORE:
                    print(f"[{tag}] below target: {best_score:.8f}")
            temp_rel = max(min_temp_rel, temp_rel * 0.992)
        else:
            no_accept += 1

            if now - last_coord > coord_interval or no_accept > 18:
                cb = coord_budget
                cb = min(cb, max(0.5, deadline - now - 5.0))
                if cb > 0.35:
                    fp, sc, cp = _coordinate_polish(f, c, cb, rng, tag + ".coord")
                    if np.isfinite(sc):
                        if sc < best_score:
                            best_score = sc
                            best_f = fp.copy()
                        if sc < float(2.0 * n * maxc) - 1e-12:
                            f = fp
                            c = cp
                            maxc = float(c.max())
                            accepted = True
                            no_accept = 0
                    last_coord = time.monotonic()

            if no_accept > 55:
                f = _perturb(best_f, rng)
                c = _autocorr(f)
                maxc = float(c.max())
                temp_rel = min(0.35, temp_rel * 2.2 + 0.008)
                no_accept = 0

            if now - last_print > 75.0:
                cur_score = float(2.0 * n * float(c.max()))
                print(f"[{tag}] iter={it} best={best_score:.8f} cur={cur_score:.8f} T={temp_rel:.3e}")
                last_print = now

    final_time = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    if final_time > 1.0:
        fp, sc, cp = _coordinate_polish(f, c, final_time - 0.5, rng, tag + ".endcoord")
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_f = fp.copy()

    print(f"[{tag}] end best={best_score:.8f}")
    return _normalize(best_f), float(best_score)


def _build_seed_pool(f0, n, rng, seed_budget):
    """Build a diversified pool of full-length starting sequences."""
    f0 = _normalize(f0)
    n = int(n)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(1.0, float(seed_budget))
    seeds = []

    _try_add_seed(seeds, "given", f0, n)
    _try_add_seed(seeds, "rev", f0[::-1], n)

    for win in (5, 11, 31, 101, 251):
        _try_add_seed(seeds, f"smooth{win}", _smooth_with_window(f0, win), n)

    for gamma in (0.55, 0.80, 1.20, 1.60):
        base = f0 + 1e-12
        _try_add_seed(seeds, f"gamma{gamma}", np.power(base, float(gamma)), n)

    _try_add_seed(seeds, "uniform", np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), n)

    for i in range(10):
        _try_add_seed(seeds, f"fourier{i}", _fourier_seed(n, rng), n)
    for i in range(6):
        _try_add_seed(seeds, f"beta{i}", _beta_seed(n, rng), n)

    for a, b in ((-0.5, 1.5), (1.5, -0.5), (-1.0, 0.2), (0.2, -1.0), (0.65, 0.65), (-0.3, -0.3)):
        _try_add_seed(seeds, f"power{a}_{b}", _power_seed(n, a, b), n)

    eb = f0.copy()
    m = max(1, eb.size)
    eb[0] += 0.25 * float(eb[0:1].mean() + 1.0 / m)
    eb[-1] += 0.25 * float(eb[-1:].mean() + 1.0 / m)
    _try_add_seed(seeds, "endpoint_boost", eb, n)

    cb = f0.copy()
    mid = m // 2
    cb[mid - 2:mid + 2] += 0.5 * (1.0 / m)
    _try_add_seed(seeds, "center_boost", cb, n)

    # Coarse continuous piecewise seeds.
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 55.0 and n > 0:
        configs = (
            (2048, 16, 18.0),
            (2048, 32, 18.0),
            (1024, 16, 12.0),
            (1024, 32, 12.0),
        )
        total_cfg = sum(x[2] for x in configs)
        scale = min(1.45, max(0.25, (remaining - 35.0) / max(1.0, total_cfg)))
        for M, K, sec0 in configs:
            sec = sec0 * scale
            if time.monotonic() + sec + 10.0 > deadline:
                break
            try:
                prof, sc = _piecewise_optimize(M, K, sec, rng, f"pc{M}_{K}")
            except Exception:
                prof = None
                sc = np.inf
            if prof is not None and np.isfinite(sc):
                _try_add_seed(seeds, f"pc{M}_{K}", prof, n)

    # A few coarse full-length block-scaling refinements of the best seeds.
    if seeds:
        seeds.sort()
        indices = (0, 1, 3)
        for idx in indices:
            if idx >= len(seeds):
                continue
            if time.monotonic() + 25.0 > deadline:
                break
            sc, _id, name, f = seeds[idx]
            Kuse = 32 if "pc" in name else 16
            budget = 20.0 if n < 12000 else 12.0
            budget = min(budget, max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 10.0))
            if budget < 3.0:
                break
            try:
                bf, bs = _block_scale_optimize(f, Kuse, budget, rng, f"block{idx}")
            except Exception:
                continue
            if bf is not None and np.isfinite(bs):
                _try_add_seed(seeds, f"block{idx}_{name}", bf, n)

    seeds.sort()
    return seeds


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Search for a non-negative coefficient sequence minimizing evaluate_sequence.
    Returns a Python list of floats.
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    total_deadline = time.monotonic() + max(120.0, float(budget_s)) - 25.0

    try:
        f0 = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        f0 = np.ones(6121, dtype=np.float64)

    if f0.size == 0:
        f0 = np.ones(6121, dtype=np.float64)
    f0 = np.nan_to_num(f0, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    np.clip(f0, 0.0, 1000.0, out=f0)

    n = int(f0.size)
    if n < 3:
        f0 = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)
        n = f0.size

    # Keep the search in a reliable size range.  The official evaluator's
    # direct convolution becomes expensive for much larger lengths.
    if n > 12000:
        f0 = _interp_to_length(f0, 12000)
        n = f0.size

    f0 = _normalize(f0)

    best_f = f0.copy()
    best_c = _autocorr(best_f)
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * float(best_c.max())) if best_c.size else np.inf
    print(f"initial n={n} score={best_score:.8f}")

    try:
        seed_budget = min(190.0, max(45.0, 0.11 * (total_deadline - time.monotonic())))
        seed_pool = _build_seed_pool(f0, n, rng, seed_budget)
        if not seed_pool:
            seed_pool = [(best_score, 0, "given", best_f)]
    except Exception:
        seed_pool = [(best_score, 0, "given", best_f)]

    # Ensure the current best is represented in the optimization list.
    if not any(item[2] == "given" for item in seed_pool):
        seed_pool.append((best_score, len(seed_pool), "given", best_f))
    seed_pool.sort()

    now = time.monotonic()
    final_reserve = max(110.0, min(520.0, 0.20 * (total_deadline - now)))
    main_deadline = total_deadline - final_reserve

    # Optimize the strongest, mutually different enough seeds.
    top = seed_pool[:4]
    weights = (0.52, 0.30, 0.13, 0.05)

    for idx, item in enumerate(top):
        if time.monotonic() >= main_deadline - 15.0:
            break
        sc, _id, name, f = item
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            continue

        remain = main_deadline - time.monotonic() - 8.0
        if remain < 25.0:
            break

        if idx >= len(weights):
            alloc = min(remain, max(15.0, remain * 0.4))
        else:
            wfrac = weights[idx] / sum(weights[idx:])
            alloc = min(remain, max(25.0, 0.94 * (main_deadline - now) * wfrac))

        # Protect the tail but leave room for final polishing.
        alloc = min(alloc, max(20.0, remain - 2.0))
        print(f"\n=== full optimize {idx+1}/{len(top)} seed={name} alloc={alloc:.1f}s current_best={best_score:.8f} ===")

        try:
            f_opt, sc_opt = _gradient_optimize(f, alloc, rng, f"full{name}")
        except Exception as exc:
            print("gradient exception:", repr(exc))
            f_opt, sc_opt = f, sc

        if np.isfinite(sc_opt) and sc_opt < best_score:
            best_score = sc_opt
            best_f = f_opt.copy()
            best_c = _autocorr(best_f)
            print(f"new best after full optimize {idx+1}: {best_score:.8f}")

    # Dedicated final coordinate polish.
    now = time.monotonic()
    final_time = max(0.0, total_deadline - now - 25.0)
    if final_time > 40.0:
        print(f"\n=== final coordinate polish budget={final_time:.1f}s ===")
        try:
            bf, bs, bc = _coordinate_polish(best_f, _autocorr(best_f), final_time, rng, "finalcoord")
            if np.isfinite(bs) and bs < best_score:
                best_score = bs
                best_f = bf.copy()
                best_c = bc
        except Exception as exc:
            print("final coordinate exception:", repr(exc))

    # If time remains, another short gradient pass and final polish.
    now = time.monotonic()
    extra = max(0.0, total_deadline - now - 15.0)
    if extra > 150.0:
        gtime = min(240.0, 0.55 * extra)
        print(f"\n=== final gradient pass budget={gtime:.1f}s ===")
        try:
            f2, s2 = _gradient_optimize(best_f, gtime, rng, "final_grad")
            if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < best_score:
                best_score = s2
                best_f = f2.copy()
                best_c = _autocorr(best_f)
        except Exception as exc:
            print("final gradient exception:", repr(exc))

        extra2 = max(0.0, total_deadline - time.monotonic() - 15.0)
        if extra2 > 50.0:
            try:
                bf, bs, bc = _coordinate_polish(best_f, _autocorr(best_f), extra2, rng, "final_coord2")
                if np.isfinite(bs) and bs < best_score:
                    best_score = bs
                    best_f = bf.copy()
                    best_c = bc
            except Exception as exc:
                print("final coord2 exception:", repr(exc))

    # Final safety normalization and clipping.
    best_f = _normalize(best_f)
    best_f = np.nan_to_num(best_f, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    np.clip(best_f, 0.0, 1000.0, out=best_f)
    best_f = best_f / max(1e-300, float(best_f.sum()))

    out = [float(x) for x in best_f]

    # Optional official evaluation, ignored if unavailable or invalid.
    try:
        official = evaluate_sequence(out)
        if np.isfinite(official):
            best_score = float(official)
    except Exception:
        pass

    print(f"\nFINAL internal/official score = {best_score:.8f}")
    print(f"FINAL target = {TARGET_SCORE}")
    return out
```
